with the ASPIRE cluster tag; that CPU-only build route also materializes the
Firmus archive (as in 913b88b). No GPU job runs from this commit.

Why: in the Prefill MegaMoE (control sf128 + swizzle, prefill-balanced-4096)
the FC1 K loop takes ~1400 clk per K block against 1024 clk of MMA work. With
four m64n128 SS warpgroups a K block reads 96 KiB of WGMMA operands from
shared memory (A 4 x 8, B 4 x 16) next to 48 KiB of TMA writes, 144 KiB or
1152 clk at 128 B/clk. Two warpgroups that each own a 64x256 row half with one
m64n256 accumulator read 80 KiB (A 2 x 8, B 2 x 32), 128 KiB with TMA = 1024
clk. Under the host's 640-thread Prefill launch the kernel-wide register cap
is 96 and ptxas rejects a real m64n256 WGMMA (C7602, needs >= 154), so the
header-only variant had to use RS m64n128 pairs, ldmatrix, setmaxnreg 200/24
and idle warpgroups; its bench did not shorten ML1Loop (683-703 us per CTA vs
679-716 for the control, at a 4% higher clock) and cost ML1Epi +22 us and
ML2Epi +11 us per CTA. The straightforward version needs the host to launch
the Prefill tile with 256 math threads: 64 + 64 + 256 = 384 threads, cap 168
registers, real m64n256 SS WGMMA, no idle warpgroups, no register hand-off.
This commit builds the image whose _C.so can do that; the header path is a
runtime overlay/bench patch, so the image's header and every unswitched JIT
source stay as on main.

What changed:
- patches/deep-gemm-host/sm90-prefill-2wg.patch (the only entry of
  patches/deep-gemm-host/series), on sgl-project/DeepGEMM v0.1.5 1c8138a5:
  get_mega_moe_config_sm90 keeps its choice (BLOCK_M 128 / BLOCK_N 256, 512
  math threads, 64 dispatch + 64 loader threads); only when
  DG_SM90_PREFILL_2WG is set non-zero and the shape is Qwen3's Prefill
  (hidden 4096, intermediate 1536, 4 ranks, the auto split-MN tile) the config
  carries 256 math threads, and the pipeline budget counts 8 instead of 16
  math warps of barriers (3 stages either way, 128 B less dynamic shared
  memory, matching the header's 24 barriers). The runtime keeps the Prefill L2 arrival
  counter for this geometry (default_split_mn_barrier_opt also for 256 math
  threads with BLOCK_M 128, a combination the stock heuristic never produces).
  Registers stay the header default for 256 math threads (template value 0:
  dispatch 48, loaders 40, math 168 = 48640 <= 64512); 168 vs 224 compiled to
  the same register use (max R154, no spill), so no host register value is
  added. The L1-output TMA box follows from the host's existing warpgroup split
  (2 x 1 warpgroups: 64 rows x 128 columns, one store per warpgroup). Unset,
  the config, the template arguments and the generated JIT source are
  unchanged (the new branch is reached only through the env check; the impl
  change only touches a geometry the stock heuristic does not return), so this
  image is a drop-in for main's serving and for the bench's image/control
  variants. Decode geometries (BLOCK_M 64) are never affected.
- Host rebuild wiring back from 82a7f54 (originally d2fd691), unchanged
  except that it now carries this host patch: containers/rebuild-deepgemm-
  host.py (rebuilds only _C.so from the pinned v0.1.5 source with the series,
  ABI import against the CUDA stub, header must stay pristine, record in
  /opt/apac/deep-gemm-host-build.json), containers/Dockerfile,
  scripts/build-sglang-container.sh (clone v0.1.5 with submodules, record the
  host patches in the image manifest), containers/build-sm90-megamoe.py (host
  record in sm90-megamoe-build.json), scripts/check-firmus-image.sh (real
  _C.so hash, host patch names/order/SHA-256, source commit, manifest) and
  qwen-experiment-metrics/tests/test_firmus_image.py. 82a7f54's pre-reduce
  host patch, header overlay, sbatch and gate changes are not included.
- patches/deep-gemm-overlay/sm90-prefill-2wg-host-m64n256.patch (header, on
  top of sm90-prefill-fc2-sf128 and sm90-prefill-fc2-swizzle; NOT yet in
  configs/firmus-framework.json deep_gemm_overlay_patches): kPrefill2WG =
  Qwen Prefill shapes, BLOCK_M 128, BLOCK_N 256, 256 math threads, reused
  accumulator (a constexpr of the template thread count, no macro). It joins
  the Prefill specializations (phase split, dynamic FC2 claims, per-128 FC2
  scales, Qwen mainloop, paired combine) and static-asserts the host flags
  that make them apply. The default warpgroup split already gives 2 x 1
  warpgroups of 64x256 and L1WGMMA = m64n256k32 SS. Edits: FC1 weight-SF row
  from n_block_idx (one gate and one up 128-channel block per 256-column tile,
  as before); FC2 two carried weight scales per warpgroup (chunks 0..15 and
  16..31 = the tile's two 128-row SF blocks); the pair amax exchange is skipped
  (one warpgroup owns the whole 128-column scale group); the FC2 scatter sends
  one 512-byte row per warp iteration with the 16 rows' source metadata loaded
  up front; the combine keeps 4 chunks of 2 KiB (8 warps x 5 buffers fit).
  Barrier counts follow from 8 math warps (stage empty 8, FC2 claim ring 1 +
  8, combine 16 mbarriers, named barriers 1 = 64 + 256, 2 = 256, 3/4 per
  warpgroup; the pair-amax barriers 12/13 are unused). Same tile, scheduler,
  pipeline, K order, FP8 operands, FP32 accumulation, scales and quantization
  granularity (FC1 output per-128 scale groups, FC2 activations per 128); no
  approximation or skipped work.
- patches/deep-gemm-bench/{control-sf128-swizzle,fc1-2wg-host-m64n256}/ for
  the later kernel bench: 01 sf128 + 02 swizzle (copies of the overlay
  patches), the candidate's 03 = the overlay patch above, its env
  (DG_SM90_PREFILL_2WG=1, needs the kernel-bench env support) and its own
  timeline patch (the stock one collides with the 2-warpgroup FC2 loop; the
  same counters are added there); the control's timeline is 7d7e5d3's. 7 Slurm
  steps.

Checks:
- Host: the patch applies to a pristine v0.1.5 checkout (1c8138a5) and
  csrc/tvm_ffi_api.cpp (which includes the patched heuristic and runtime)
  passes g++ -std=c++17 -fsyntax-only with torch/tvm-ffi/CUDA 13 headers from
  a local venv; link and load happen in the image build.
- Header with the image's CUDA 13.0.88 on the Firmus login node (image
  39126563684 include tree; the image header ff7a58cc + sf128 + swizzle + this
  patch applies cleanly): Prefill 512-thread instantiation SASS
  080b4898bf885932 = control (96 registers, spill 20/20 B as before); Prefill
  2-warpgroup instantiation (256 math threads, register template 0) 168
  registers, 0 B spill, no stack, max R154 in the math region, SASS
  f64bcc5ee6f347c1; Decode SwapAB 5bcd675b848f5341 and non-swap
  14be1261f0978108 identical to control. Timeline builds: 512-thread Prefill
  1bd0eee20ebc5d11 = control timeline, 2-warpgroup 168 registers 0 spill
  (R162), Decode identical (4ca6581772679996, 20384d089e9a090b). The stock
  header cannot compile the 2-warpgroup instantiation (static assert on the
  scatter width), so DG_SM90_PREFILL_2WG without the overlay fails at JIT
  compile instead of running a wrong path.
- Staging the bench layout against the real image header reproduces exactly
  the compiled candidate and timeline headers.
- bash -n, the line-continuation grep, the kernel-experiment tag check, ruff
  and the full pytest suite pass.

Not run: the _C.so rebuild itself (first exercised by this build, as the same
machinery did in 82a7f54's build), and anything on a GPU. After the build the
image pin in configs/firmus-framework.json moves to it; then the kernel bench
(gate with the switch, both variants' timelines) decides.

Expected: if the shared-memory model of the FC1 deficit holds, ML1Loop -20 to
-60 us per CTA; against that, 8 instead of 16 warps for the FC1/FC2 epilogues
(the RS variant measured ML1Epi +22 us, ML2Epi +11 us per CTA), the FC2 scatter
and the combine (85 us phase). Net kernel -3% to +3%. If ML1Loop does not
drop with a real m64n256 SS either, the shared-memory bandwidth explanation is
refuted.

1152 clk at 128 B/clk. Two warpgroups that each own a 64x256 row half with one
m64n256 accumulator read 80 KiB (A 2 x 8, B 2 x 32), 128 KiB with TMA = 1024
clk. Under the host''s 640-thread Prefill launch the kernel-wide register cap
